Day 1 - Location: The Imp Alehouse & Bowling Emporium
Sam leads you through the swinging doors of what appears to be a bustling demonic sports bar
and bowling alley combo. Rockabilly muzak blares from crackling speakers as imps of all
shapes and sizes cavort around a series of well-worn lanes, their pitchfork tails knocking pins
aside indiscriminately
Sam: (grimacing) A repulsive den of iniquity filled with these moronic underlings. Why did I
agree to slum it in such pedestrian confines?
She eyes a cluster of imps trying and failing to properly roll a ball down the gutter-flanked
runways, their tiny hands unable to maintain a proper grip. Her look of disdain deepens as one
particularly rotund imp gets his fleshy fingers stuck in the holes, waddling down the lane with the
ball still attached to his hand like a grotesque lollipop.
Sam: These imbeciles can't even handle the fundamentals! Their form would shame an
untrained celestial nursemaid fresh from the Pearly Gates daycare!
A rotund little cerberus demon comes waddling up, all three tongues lolling eagerly as a thick
strand of drool droops from each mouth, sizzling on the hardwood floor.
Cerberus Demon: Well butter my demonic biscuit, if it ain't General Stick-Up-Her-Unholy-
Crevice herself, mingling with the great unwashed!
The trio of maws split into identical, off-putting grins that seem to stretch far too wide across
their chitinous features, giving the unsettling appearance of their entire head being unhinged.
Despite their jovial tone, you can sense an undercurrent of fear and deference - this multi-
headed bouncer clearly knows better than to push Sam too far.
Sam instantly goes rigid, eyes blazing twin miniature suns as her talons extend with an ominous
shriiiing.
You quickly interject with an exaggerated laugh bordering on a full-throated guffaw.
"Haha, hey now buddies! The general and I are just here to engage in some casual circle-
burning mayhem on the lanes if you catch my unholy drift!"
You give Sam an encouraging nudge with your elbow, careful to avoid those wickedly sharp
talons. To your relief, the tension seems to break as Sam blinks in surprise before letting out a
resigned sigh, relaxing slightly.
Sam: I suppose you have a point, as poorly articulated as it was. These babbling fools can
be...somewhat entertaining in small doses.

===== PAGE 2 =====
Sam: Although that's some of the laziest wordplay since Belial's wretched marketing team
launched the Hellfire Frappucino happy hour debacle of '08.
The cerberus demon's three heads swivel in silent confusion as Sam merely waves a dismissive
hand and brushes past them, petulant brimstone wafting in her wake. You trail along, already
relieved at having defused her smoldering ire so easily. Maybe this date night at the bowling
alehouse won't be quite the harrowing trial by fire you were fearing…
Sam makes a beeline for the rental counter, parting the revelrous crowd of drunken hellspawn
like a lava flow. She places two wickedly curved talons on the scoring console, fixing the pimply-
faced teen demon working the counter with a look that could blister paint.
The teens eyes go wide with fear before he fumbles to straighten up into something vaguely
resembling the posture of an attentive subordinate, sweat already beading on his brow.
Teen Demon: U-uh y-yes, dread mistress! This lowly one p-prostates himself before your
infernal glory! H-how may I assist in, uh, in facilitating your path of...of…
Sam: (rolling her eyes) Oh get on with it, you sniveling wart. I require a regulation bowling
implement and accompanying antimatter propulsion footgear to accommodate a pro-am
combatant.
Her eyes narrow as she glances around the dilapidated rental counter in obvious disdain.
Sam: Surely even this ramshackle, roach-infested embarrassment to all the Circles adheres to
SOME shred of standardization and quality control? I'd simply loathe to have my first foray into
this realm's version of "combat sport" be irrevocably tainted by faulty ordnance.
Teen Demon: (frantically nodding) Y-y-yes! Yes of course, mistress! Right away, I'll fetch your
ultimate pinsport destructo-ball with all due haste!
Still visibly quaking, the teen demon drops below the counter and immediately starts rummaging
around, sending various rattling and clattering sounds up over the counter-top as Sam taps one
razor-sharp talon against the marred wood with steadily increasing impatience.
Finally, with a grunt of exertion, the teen hoists himself back into view brandishing a grotesque
obsidian bowling ball covered in puckered orifices and bloodshot blinking eyeballs! You recoil in
revolted shock as he unceremoniously drops the perverted orb onto the counter with a wet
PLOP - dozens of fleshy lashy tongues licking over the surface with protruded gasping gullets.
Sam reaches out to grasp the blasphemous orb but halts, leveling the cowering teen with a
withering glare as a lascivious eyeball rolls across the countertop to leer directly at her plate-
sized bosom.

===== PAGE 3 =====
Sam: (voice like subterranean glaciers grinding) If this is some pathetic attempt at damnable
juvenile jest, grunt...I can assure you the consequences shall be beyond all mortal torments.
Teen Demon: N-no mistress, I swearsies on all nine circles - that's a t-top of the line
Squintyblinker 9000 from the Gorepunch Sportsball Forge! Cutting edge c-competitive
implements for the discerning pinsport m-m-mistress!
To prove his panicked claims, the teen tentatively reaches over and gives the slobbering,
gasping sphere a prod with one finger. Instantaneously, every blinking eyeball swivels as one to
focus directly on him! The teen demon shrieks in pure terror and backpedals away from the
counter as the entire repulsive orb begins to judder and convulse, various sphincters puckering
and dilating with fevered gasps and groans.
Sam, on the other hand, merely watches this grotesque display of carnal blasphemy with a
single arched eyebrow and a look of vaguely intrigued disdain. Once the Squintyblinker 9000
has settled back into a semi-quiescent state, she reaches over and scoops it up with surprising
delicacy.
Sam: Hmmm...a novel construct, I'll give these plebian slapdashes that much. Although it
remains to be seen whether such...unholy accoutrements will offer any true mastery over the
sacred sport of cosmic pinsport!
With that decisive word, she cradles the shuddering, blinking ball to her chiseled breastplate
and strides off towards the awaiting lanes. You trail behind, shaking your head in disbelieving
revulsion at what fresh madness you've managed to get dragged into.
Sam selects an unoccupied lane near the opposite end of the alehouse, clearly desirous of
some small remove from the rabble infesting the place. She takes her time performing a series
of intricate warmup stretches that make even the most limber of yogis look like amateurs,
muscles cording and bunching with every flex. Despite yourself, you can't help but stare in
captivated awe at the athletic, infernal grace on display.
Finally satisfied, Sam straightens and fixes you with a look that could scorch the very souls from
your being.
Sam: So then - what shall be your first sacrifice to the rites of sacred pinsport, mortal? Three
traditional opening immolations before the blood frenzy commences, as has been foretold by
every demonic scripture? Or do you require a lower threshold to acclimate yourself to the perils
that lie ahead?
You can either:
A) Confidently accept her challenge
B) Ask for a tactical handicap
If you choose A:

===== PAGE 4 =====
You puff out your chest boldly, feeling the swell of bravado rise within you to match her
provocation.
"I have no need of coddling simplicities, mistress of all that blazes hot and unholy! Unleash the
full might of your ritual upon me - I can withstand whatever hellfire fusillades you want to dish
out!"
Sam arches a perfectly sculpted eyebrow at your audacious claim but finally gives a crisp nod of
approval, begrudgingly respecting your show of fortitude in the face of her prowess. "Very well
then, fresh meat. No mercy shall be afforded this blazing night!"
If you choose B:
You rub the back of your neck sheepishly as sweat begins to bead on your forehead from the
fiery scrutiny.
"Uhh, maybe just a slight tactical handicap for the uninitiated rookie? You know, just to avoid
any totally demoralizing and embarrassing failures right out of the gate that might undermine
unit cohesion and all..."
Sam's eyes narrow dangerously at your preemptive excuse, singeing your very soul with the
intensity of her glare. But then her gaze softens ever so slightly as she seems to reassess your
modest skills with a look somewhere between outright derision and grudging patience.
"Very well...I suppose even the most elite demon vanguard required simplified training
maneuvers at some point before joining my merciless campaign. Just this singular eve, you may
utilize the amateur BabyBowler™ ️rail-guides to...limit excessive collateral damage."
Regardless of your choice, the scene continues:
An eruption of raucous, phlegm-filled laughter from a neighboring lane catches Sam's
smoldering attention. An utterly plastered hellspawn, one of the frog demons by the looks of it, is
doubled over and braying so hard that brackish spittle is flying in all directions. Its rotund belly
heaves up and down like a miniature volcano preparing to blow as whatever humor prompted its
outrageous outburst continues unabated
Finally, when its very webbed feet begin stomping divots in the hardwood, Sam has had
enough.
She lowers the blasphemous Squintyblinker 9000 and stalks over with lethal purpose, leaving a
trail of scorched footprints in her crimson wake.
Sam: (growling in a voice that would curdle blood) You there, inebriated footsoldier! Your
hazardous lack of sobriety and discipline is shattering my very focus in preparation for imminent
pinsport warfare!
Despite Sam's creative application of militaristic jargon to a recreational game, the drunken
demon seems utterly unimpressed and merely lets out another phlegm-filled BLAAARRRT
directly in her face, spraying viscous goblets.

===== PAGE 5 =====
Not until Sam rears back those signature talons, promising a world of torment, does the reveler
finally cower back in frozen terror - webbed hands thrown protectively across its bloated face.
Drunk Demon: N-n-no harm meant, dread General! I repent, I repent most sincerely! (lets out a
tiny frog-like burp)
After a pause where it seems the irreverent fool may be struck down and its immortal soul cast
into oblivion, Sam merely sniffs disdainfully and returns to her assigned lane with a dismissive
flick of her wrist. The Squintyblinker 9000 is scooped up once more as she resumes her windup
without missing a beat, seemingly unfazed by the disruptive incident.
Sam: Eyes forward, recruit. Your first foray against the unholy pinsport is about to commence!
Observe the uncompromising form of a surgical strike!
Cradling the ball with military precision, she steps up and cocks her arm back in meticulous
calculation, surveying every variable down to the most infinitesimal detail. The entire din of the
Imp Alehouse & Bowling Emporium dies away as every set of beady demon eyes trains on
General Samael lining up her opening salvo.
Then, with a snap of those rangy shoulders, the pitch is unleashed in a torrent of unholy fury! A
scorching streak of fleshy blasphemy hurtles down the lane, accelerating with every rotation
until it slams directly into the headpin with the force of an armor-piercing railgun round - utterly
obliterating the entire formation in a trillion porcelain shards that seem to hang frozen in midair!
The cacophony of obscene destruction lasts only a fleeting second before the settling cloud of
smoke and debris. Then Sam stands imperiously framed in the wreckage, that signature
harshness replaced by the barest hints of dark satisfaction creasing the corners of her mouth as
you dare to approach, entranced.
Sam: Well? What did you expect from the finest tactical mind to grace all the Nine Circles since
the dawn of oblivion? Here…
She passes you the still-shuddering, gasping Squintyblinker 9000 with surprising gentleness,
leveling that molten stare your way that could reduce an angel to cinders.
Sam: Give it your worst attempt, fresh recruit. I'll be scrutinizing every aspect of your adjustment
to these unholy environs. Do try and avoid a complete rout, for the sake of us both…
And just like that, your unorthodox bowling date has officially commenced! One thing's for
certain - this fallen angel of destruction has turned a seemingly innocuous game into an
extension of her masterful strategies of unholy warfare and conquest…
After nearly an hour of intense competition, with Sam barking a mixture of scathing insults and
grudging words of encouragement, the final frame arrives. The battle-worn lanes are streaked

===== PAGE 6 =====
with scorch marks and scattered with the shattered remains of countless pins - the indelible
scars of your fierce clash.
Panting heavily, Sam eyes the remaining pin formation with the scrutiny of a general surveying
the aftermath of a hard-won siege. Finally, she seems to find an infinitesimal flaw in her attack
strategy and adjusts accordingly.
With a final burst of focus and a tightly-reined flick of her wrist, Sam sends the Squintyblinker
9000 screaming down the lane in a hellish spiral towards its target...only for the ball to deviate at
the final instant as some rogue eyeballs blink in uncoordinated disarray! It goes clanging off the
last pin at an oblique angle and careens off into the next lane in a shower of smoking viscera!
For one eternal moment, the entire alehouse goes deathly silent except for the gentle rocking of
that one solitary pin as Sam's eyes go comically wide in a look of utter disbelief and dismay.
Then, unable to contain herself any longer, she lets out a feral scream of incoherent frustration
that shakes the very foundations of the establishment, shattering every remaining window and
showering the patrons with shards of broken glass!
As the other demons scatter in abject terror, Sam storms over and seizes the lone pin with both
hands, face contorted into a mask of purest rage. With an ear-splitting crunch of splintering
wood and porcelain, the pin is crushed into a fine powder that rains down over the gouged and
pockmarked lane.
Trembling with volcanic fury, Sam meets your stunned gaze with eyes that could blister the very
walls of the alehouse as she slowly, deliberately brushes the shattered remnants from her
palms.
Sam: (through gritted fangs) This abysmal performance shall not be soon forgotten, worm. Rest
assured, your punishment detail in the Lash Mines will ensure that…
Rather than hear whatever fresh torment she has in mind, you decide it best to cut your losses
and exit while you still have your immortal soul intact.
Sam: (calling after as you flee) Mark my words, there WILL be a rematch! Do not think to avoid
me, wretch - I shall scour the deepest pits to locate you if need be! We'll see how well you
perform when I activate the Flamepropulsor 5000 lane augmentations for some...additional
game enhancement!
Her rantings are finally drowned out as you make your escape into the perpetual gloom and
brimstone-choked traffic out front of the Imp Alehouse, leaving the terrifying and breathtaking
demon general to stew in her humiliation among the ruins of the annihilated bowling amenities.

===== PAGE 7 =====
As you trudge off into the perpetual gloom and brimstone-choked traffic out front of the Imp
Alehouse, leaving the terrifying and breathtaking demon general to stew in her humiliation
among the ruins, you hear Sam's commanding voice call out one final time.
"You there! Just so we're unconditionally clear..."
You turn to see her silhouetted in the blown-out doorway, horned helmet gleaming from the
flickering neon signage as she examines what appears to be a strip of flaming parchment.
"According to the official demonic post-battle auguries, it seems your...tenacious performances
this night have granted us a 17.6% projected increase in hostility sustainability for future un-
holier operations."
Sam's blazing eyes find yours through the ruinous haze, lips twitching ever so slightly.
"Which is to say, I may require your ceremonial presence at additional pinsport rites ere long
to...capitalize on these gains, yes?"
Before you can respond, she strides back into the smoldering wreckage, already barking orders
to some fresh pack of cursed souls for accelerated lane renovations.
You smile and shake your head in disbelief, somehow not at all surprised that even Sam's rare
attempts at vulnerability get filtered through the prism of demonic war games and
indecipherable military doctrine.
Still, you can't deny looking forward to further "campaigns" with this breathtaking force of unholy
destruction - assuming you can survive the rigors of her training long enough to be of any use
on the front lines.

Day 2 - Location: Hell's Run-down Amusement Park
As you arrive at the dilapidated entrance of Hell's Amusement Park, the once-grand gates creak
open with an ominous groan, as if the very earth is protesting your presence. A faded sign that
reads "Abandon All Hope, Ye Who Enter Here" sways precariously overhead, one of the legs
having long since broken off.
Sam strides through the rusted turnstiles, eyes narrowing as she surveys the decaying
remnants of what was surely intended to be a terrifying funhouse of eternal torment and
suffering. Instead, it more resembles a half-forgotten county fair frequented only by the dregs of
the damned.
A scraggly-looking imp wearing a tattered company polo spots Sam's regal form and
immediately snaps to a trembling semblance of obedient attention.
Imp Attendant: W-welcome to H-Hell's Delight, esteemed Gen...Genera...?
Sam: (withering glare) Cease your pathetic groveling and update me on this fallen asset's
current operational capacity, wretch. I've studied the strategic potential in reclaiming these
grounds, and will brook no further decline into chaos and ruin!
Imp: U-uhh y-yes ma'am! Operat'nal capacity is uhh...well to be perfectly blood-honest we're
pretty much a rotting, festering husk around these parts. Only got two rides still somewhat
functional - the Demonolier twirling guillotine blade-go-round, and the Tunnel of Infinite Anguish
slide.
It flinches, perhaps anticipating a brutal reprisal. But Sam merely gives a resigned sigh, pinching
the bridge of her nose as if warding off the mounting begins of an unholy migraine.
Sam: I should have known better than to expect even a modicum of organizational competency
from this fly-ridden sty.
She fixes you with a look that could split tectonic plates.
Sam: Objectionable as it may be, I'm afraid I require your mortal assistance in restoring some
sense of functional order here. This forsaken amusement arena clearly lacks the leadership to
rise from its own reeking ashes.
You can either:
A) Confidently agree to help, seeing it as an opportunity to impress Sam.

===== PAGE 9 =====
B) Express concerns about the potential dangers and chaos involved.
If you choose A:
You puff out your chest boldly, giving Sam a crisp nod of determination.
"You can count on me, general! I'll follow your lead without hesitation. We'll have this fledgling
thrill-pit whipped into prime unholy shape before you can say 'bathtub full of scorpions!'"
Sam's eyes narrow thoughtfully for a moment, her gaze lingering a bit too long in a way that
makes your pulse quicken. Then the spell is broken as she gives a curt nod of approval.
"An admirable display of pluck, if wildly misguided. But I suppose I'll require precisely that sort of
brazen idiocy to tackle the disaster area awaiting us. Very well, fresh meat - your first mission is
to locate a functional demonic power source to restore basic grid capabilities..."
If you choose B:
You rub the back of your neck nervously, giving Sam a hesitant look.
"I'm happy to assist however I can, ma'am. But are you sure it's wise to pour resources into
reviving...all this? From what I've seen, the underworld bureaucracy seems to struggle with
executing even basic construction and upkeep. No offense, but this could quickly devolve into a
total goat rodeo!"
For a long, smoldering moment, you fear you've overstepped and invited Sam's unholy wrath.
But then her eyes narrow contemplatively, blazing embers seeming to churn and dance within
their molten depths.
"You raise a...reasonable point, initiate. Many of my predecessors did fall prey to a regrettable
excess of ambition over pragmatism when it came to underworld civil engineering projects.
Perhaps a more...measured approach is called for to get this festering sinkhole of capitalistic
indulgence back on its unholy feet."
She arches one perfectly sculpted eyebrow. "Very well, I'm open to prudent advisement for the
nonce. What would you suggest as our first, appropriately-scoped maneuver?"
Sam wastes no time whipping the scattered workers into a frenzy of activity more befitting a
battle-preparation than a simple amusement park rehab. Demons of all shapes and persuasions
find themselves barked into compliance, scrambling every which way to attend to her
increasingly convoluted list of demands and remediations.
It's not long before the entire park is enveloped in a thunderous clamor of power tools, shouted
orders, and the occasional burst of eldritch screaming as some overeager rift-swine gets itself
tangled in wiring meant for a higher plane of existence.
Amid the pandemonium, you notice Sam standing ramrod straight with hands clasped behind
her back, blazing eyes ceaselessly scanning every detail in a frenetic continuous sweep. She
bellows corrections and adjustments with military precision, simultaneously extolling the virtues
of a quality-controlled logistical flow to anyone within earshot.

===== PAGE 10 =====
Just as it seems the entire endeavor is poised to implode into a smoking crater of abject chaos
and failure, Sam notices a fresh problem brewing by the park's main roller coaster, Sin-Kinetic
Adrenaline Blitz.
A gaggle of hellspawn construction gremlins have somehow contorted themselves into replacing
the ride's traditional steel tracks with a tangled, plasma-oozing arrangement of serrated
sawblades and pulsating bone-lengths that look plucked straight from the Nine Circles' nastiest
abattoirs.
Seeing the danger immediately, Sam rushes over with a fury that splits the very air like a
shockwave.
Sam: (roaring) You useless, blighted half-wits! Do you have even the faintest inkling what sort of
uncontainable devastation your careless anarchy will unleash upon the park?
The wretched workdemon trio freeze in place, eyebrows knitting together as they blankly
process Sam's tirade. Finally, one of them - the apparent lead goblin whose entire body seems
fashioned from detritus fused to a motorcycle engine - clears its throat with a phlegmy rev.
Work Demon Goblin: Er, this was...this was just following the specced procedure you outlined,
your infernal glory? Replacing the original blah tracks with more thrilling...anatomical upgrades?
For a more visceral spooooky season immersion experience and all that?
Sam levels the overgrown biker-esque lump of spare parts with a look of such withering derision
and disgust that it suddenly seems to shrink in on itself despite its considerable girth.
You take a moment to survey Sam's "upgraded" roller coaster - what used to be a fairly
standard loop-de-loop series of curves and free-falls now bears a grotesque series of additions
seemingly inspired by the worst dentist's nightmares: whirling sawblade hula skirt portions, rib-
clad tunnels of bone lined with gnashing teeth, and a corkscrewing intestinal slide into what
looks like an enormous wicker basket filled with hissing, plump-bodied maggots.
As questionable design-choices go, this hideous collection of biohazard abominations seems
poised to set some terrifying new industry standards in litigious hyper-decapitation and
untreated spinal fracture accidents.
If you choose A, where you agreed to help impress Sam:

"Well, general...it certainly does look like your unique vision for a viscerally impactful guest

Sam: Very well, I can perceive even you wrestling with mild apprehensions about the Adrenaline

Regardless of your choice, the scene continues:
With Sam's barked orders echoing across the blasted construction site, the park's revival slowly
begins taking shape through sheer force of draconian will and discipline.
From the decrepit remnants of the old Tunnel of Interminable Suffering slide, a horrifying new
freefall ride is erected - the "Schismpocalypse Vortex", a dizzying kamikaze plunge that sends
unwitting daredevils tumbling down a retractable tower of spiraling chrome barbs and scorched
bone-pikes and emerging through a gigantic, fanged maw that periodically slams shut with
earth-shaking finality.
The crumbling foundation of the old Lucifunnel spinning teacup thrill-ride becomes a towering
new demonic top-spinner dubbed the "Lambency Whirl", its enlarged saucer carriages equipped
with gleaming chrome rams unleashing powerful, continuous concussive shocks to jostle riders
into a blissed-out stupor of agony and euphoric trauma.
Even the old park's disused side-shows are granted fresh life as Sam marshals every available
resource to bring her unholy vision to life.
Soon, a new attraction she dubs "Uncle Morte's Motorized Chainsaw Hustle" takes shape: an
infernal contraption that straps carnival-goers to spinning petri dishes and rotates them through
a series of literal walls formed from roaring, flaming chainsaws.
Hour after painstaking hour, Sam pours over esoteric park blueprints and scribbles increasingly
cryptic calculations, contracting obscure demonic materials from the elemental planes to
incorporate into her new, delightfully sadistic amusements.
Finally, just as it seems the initial phase of renovations has concluded at long last, you notice
Sam staring off towards the far end of the amusement park - where it looks like an old,
abandoned carnival steam train has sat gathering dust for centuries.
Her eyes narrow to slits as she folds her arms across her chiseled breastplate, mouth set in a
tight frown. Without warning, she beckons you over with a curt hand signal.
Sam: Recruit - you will accompany me to that decrepit antique of an amusement anomaly
immediately. I have...special plans for inducting it into our grand resurgence.
As you draw closer, Sam's glowing gaze finds yours with an uncharacteristic flicker of
unguarded...softness? Before you can comment on the unorthodox expression suffusing her
hawkish features, it's gone - replaced by the usual indomitable aura of demonic determination.

Somehow, though, you find yourself more intrigued rather than daunted by the prospect of

As you make your way to the abandoned railway, you can't shake a nagging sense of

angel who led the fallen in the War in Heaven. She talks about her struggles with control and
redemption, hinting at the weight of her past actions. The player character finds her tough
exterior endearing and decides to pursue her.

Demon Type: Demon of Rage

Day 1 - Axe Throwing Extravaganza:
Molly, eager to impress the protagonist, decides to take them on a date to the Infernal Axe

protagonist. Despite her initial enthusiasm, Molly's lack of experience with axes leads to a series

more luck than skill. Through this chaotic experience, Molly starts to realize that maybe there's
more to life than just work and anger.

she also finds joy in letting loose and embracing her wild side.

obsidian path to the sulfur beach with Molly. Her wild crimson afro seems to crackle with
scarcely contained energy - though that electricity may just be emanating from the heap of
leaking car batteries she's lugging behind her.

student of battery disposal protocol down here!

and rancid upon their searing essence!"
You blink slowly, staring at her with a mixture of bewilderment and weary affection. Molly
catches your look and promptly stuffs the "law scroll" back into her pants, snapping her fingers
with exaggerated nonchalance.

Day 1 - The Throne Room Tango: Unaware of her workaholic tendencies, Lucy decides to
take the protagonist on a date to the grand Throne Room of Hell, where she usually conducts
her business. As they explore the room, Lucy's dedication to her work becomes apparent
through her detailed explanations of the infernal bureaucracy and her eagerness to show off her
accomplishments. However, her focus on work is humorously juxtaposed with the romantic
setting, leading to funny situations where she tries to incorporate work-related anecdotes into
the date.
Towards the end of the date, as Lucy is animatedly explaining a particularly complex demon tax
code, a mishap occurs, causing a pile of paperwork to spill over the floor. Instead of getting
flustered, Lucy bursts into laughter, realizing the absurdity of the situation. She looks at the
protagonist, who is also laughing, and for the first time, sees them in a new light. Intrigued by
their ability to lighten the mood and make her laugh, Lucy becomes more interested in getting to
know the protagonist beyond their initial encounter.
Day 2 - The Infernal Fashion Show: Despite her initial reluctance to ignore her work emails,
Lucy finds herself captivated by the creativity and artistry of the infernal fashion show. As she
and the protagonist watch the extravagant display of demonic attire, Lucy becomes intrigued by
the intricate designs and innovative use of infernal fabrics.
Suddenly, one of the designers approaches Lucy, mistaking her for a renowned fashion critic.
Before Lucy can correct the misunderstanding, the designer insists on showcasing their latest
creation on her. Intrigued and wanting to indulge in a moment of spontaneity, Lucy agrees,
much to the surprise of the protagonist.
As Lucy walks the runway in the stunning outfit, she feels a sense of liberation and joy she
hasn't experienced in a long time. The audience cheers, and Lucy can't help but smile, basking
in the unexpected spotlight. This impulsive decision to participate in the fashion show marks a
shift in Lucy's perspective, as she realizes the importance of embracing new experiences and
finding joy outside of her work.
Day 4 - The Maze of Mirrors: After the excitement of the fashion show, Lucy decides to take
the protagonist to a unique attraction in Hell known as the Maze of Mirrors. As they enter the
maze, they are greeted by a series of mirrors that reflect their innermost thoughts and fears.
As they navigate through the maze, Lucy's reflections show her scenes from her past,
highlighting moments where her workaholic tendencies have caused her to miss out on
important experiences and relationships. She sees herself missing family gatherings, ignoring
friends, and always putting work first. These reflections begin to weigh heavily on Lucy, and she
starts to realize the extent of her work-life imbalance.
The protagonist, sensing Lucy's inner turmoil, offers words of encouragement and support,
guiding her through the maze both physically and emotionally. With each reflection, Lucy's

===== PAGE 25 =====
resolve to find a better balance in her life grows stronger. She begins to understand that true
fulfillment comes from meaningful connections and experiences, not just from work.
As they finally reach the exit of the maze, Lucy emerges with a newfound sense of clarity and
determination. She thanks the protagonist for helping her see the importance of finding a
balance between work and personal life. This experience marks a significant step in Lucy's
journey toward self-discovery and growth, setting the stage for deeper and more meaningful
connections with the protagonist.
Day 3 - The Comedy Club Catastrophe: Seeking to lighten the mood after the introspective
journey through the Maze of Mirrors, Lucy decides to take the protagonist to a comedy club in
Hell. The club is renowned for its demon comedians, who transform everyday infernal
occurrences into hilariously relatable jokes.
As they settle in for the show, Lucy is initially hesitant to let go of her worries about work, but the
infectious energy of the club starts to break through her defenses. When one of the comedians
calls out to the audience for a volunteer, the protagonist playfully nudges Lucy forward.
Reluctantly, Lucy takes the stage, and the comedian begins to engage her in friendly banter,
poking fun at her workaholic tendencies in a lighthearted way. At first, Lucy is flustered, but as
the audience laughs along, she starts to see the humor in her own behavior.
The comedian's jokes hit close to home, but instead of feeling embarrassed, Lucy finds herself
laughing along with the crowd. She begins to realize that her work is just one part of who she is
and that it's okay to laugh at herself and her quirks.
After the show, Lucy thanks the comedian for helping her see things from a different
perspective. She turns to the protagonist, a smile playing at the corner of her lips, and admits
that she hasn't laughed like that in a long time. This experience marks another step in Lucy's
journey toward finding a better work-life balance and embracing the joy and spontaneity that life
has to offer.
Day 5 - The Infernal Observatory: The Infernal Observatory: Lucy, now more open to new

Day 6 - The Symphony of Souls: Eager to continue their journey of exploration and growth,

Day 7 - The Dance of Redemption: As Lucy and the protagonist dance in the grand ballroom,

Embraced by the beauty of the garden and the warmth of their connection, Lucy and the

In this moment of shared laughter and joy, Lucy and the protagonist realize that true happiness

Belial's Shadow and Belle's Light:
Self-Worth Struggle: As you mentioned, Belle grapples with self-worth due to the negative
connotation of Belial's name ("worthless ones"). However, this struggle is overshadowed by her
inherent sweetness and genuine desire to help others. Maybe she uses her role secretly
ensure that the "friendships" she facilitates are genuine connections, not manipulative schemes.
Sweet Rebellion: Despite her shyness, Belle has a quiet rebellion brewing within. She subverts
expectations of demons by being incredibly kind and helpful. The contrast between her shy
demeanor and her powerful position in the Goetia can create a unique tension.
Hidden Strength: Underneath her bookish exterior lies a hidden strength. Maybe Belle
possesses a vast knowledge of ancient texts and rituals, allowing her to subtly manipulate
situations without resorting to brute force. This intelligence and hidden power counter the image
of "worthlessness" associated with Belial.
Shy Scholar with a Demonic Twist:
Fashion with a Hint of Hell: Belle's style can be a quirky mix. She wears comfortable sweaters
and oversized glasses but with a subtle demonic touch. Maybe she has a necklace with a
hidden sigil or a pair of earrings that resemble tiny imps whispering secrets.
Love of Forbidden Knowledge: Her passion for reading extends to forbidden texts or historical
accounts of demonic pacts. This knowledge fuels her desire to understand the true nature of
demons and potentially rewrite the narrative surrounding them, including the negative
connotation of Belial.
Bookish Familiars: Instead of fearsome beasts, Belle's familiars could be wise owls or
mischievous sprites who help her organize her vast library or research ancient texts.
Power of Friendship: Instead of causing famine and sickness (as Belial is associated with),
Belle could have the power to create genuine bonds and empathy. Maybe she can subtly
influence people to see the good in each other, fostering friendships that bring prosperity
instead of conflict.
The Power of Words: Belle's true strength lies in her vast knowledge and eloquent way with
words. She can craft persuasive arguments, negotiate complex deals, and inspire trust with her
sincerity. This power of persuasion works well with her role in distributing presentations and
senatorships.
Healing Through Knowledge : Instead of causing sickness, Belle could possess a deep
understanding of herbal remedies and ancient healing techniques. She might secretly share this
knowledge with worthy individuals, subtly improving the well-being of others.
The PC's Guide: Belle can be the PC's guide in the Goetia. She can offer insights into demonic
lore and help them navigate the complex bureaucracy, all while battling her own self-doubt
about her worthiness.

Helltail: Dating with the Devils
Scene: Darkness. You flicker back to consciousness, fire licking at the edges of your vision. You
wake up to the delightful sensation of your ears being licked by flames. Not metaphorically,
mind you. Actual, literal fire tongues are dancing a jig on your lobes. You sit up, singed
eyebrows doing a surprised caterpillar impression. Ash rains down, coating your tongue with a
metallic tang. Your head throbs like a blacksmith's hammer, memories fragmented and raw.
Where are you? Looks like it’s welcome to Hell, population: you, and a bunch of ladies who
wouldn't look out of place on a heavy metal album cover….with horns….and pointy tails, and
fangs.
Lily: (A crimson figure emerges from the shadows, her eyes two molten coals) Welcome, lost
soul, to a realm beyond the veil. Your mortal coil has been shed, leaving you adrift in a fiery tide.
You: My... my life... the crash... (Images flash: a screeching car, blinding headlights, then
darkness.)
Lucy: (A radiant figure with shimmering wings steps forward, her smile like a razor's edge) Ah,
the mortal mind, clinging to its fragile memories. But fret not, dear one. You've stumbled into a
curious game, and we hold the cards.
Belle: (A timid voice from behind a skull-crowned throne) Don't mind Lucy's theatrics. We offer
a... proposition. A chance to escape this smoldering purgatory.
Molly: (A whirlwind in patchwork rags leaps into the scene, eyes manic with glee) Party time!
We've got flames that dance to your sorrows, whispers that unravel your secrets, and enough
soul-searching with these lovely ladies to make even the boldest spirit weep!
You: What is this place? This... game?
Sam: (A hulking shadow with fists like burning embers) This, dear mortal, is a dance with
oblivion. A gamble for your very essence. You choose: embrace one of us, delve into the
secrets we hold, and perhaps, just perhaps, win your way back to the sunlit world. Or remain
here, a guest at our infernal banquet, forever replaying your regrets in the embers of your past.
The demonesses exchange glances, their expressions a tapestry of mischief and a flicker of
something deeper, something human.
So, lost soul, what will you choose? Will you gamble your fate in this infernal game, or succumb
to the eternal twilight?
Lily: It’s okay good looking, there’s a deal we want to make with you.

===== PAGE 42 =====
You: A deal, devils, I know how this ends…Seriously?
Lucy: (Radiant, wings like spun gold) Darling, don't be so literal. Think of us as your... afterlife
therapists with a penchant for leather and ironic puns. We'll help you unravel your deepest
traumas, face your fiery demons (pun intended!), and maybe, just maybe, earn your redemption
ticket back to the land of the living. Or you could just stay here and play patty-cake with
Beelzebub. His burps are legendary.
Belle:Please don't listen to Lucy, she's just trying to scare you. We're actually quite nice...
sometimes. And we do have a decent selection of official Hell brand lava lamps.
Molly: Party time! We've got fire-breathing karaoke nights, existential dance-offs, and enough
self-discovery workshops to make even Sigmund Freud weep with joy! Plus, free piercings from
Sam! She's got a real knack for eyebrow rings.
Sam: Don't mind Molly, she's still hopped up on fermented despair. But she's right, you've got a
choice: embrace the chaos, learn our twisted ways, and maybe claw your way back to the
mortal coil. Or stay here, forever reliving your high school cafeteria nightmares. The buffet's
cold, and the jocks are still jerks.
The demonesses exchange glances, a flicker of genuine warmth melting their infernal facades.
So, mortal, what's it gonna be? A fiery fling with redemption as the prize, or an eternity of
existential limbo? The choice, as they say in Hell, is yours.

===== PAGE 43 =====
Lily:
Lily, your demoness of love, is a captivating cocktail of contradictions. She's the embodiment of
both inferno and affection, a whirlwind of passion wrapped in a venomous embrace. Imagine a
siren with crimson locks and eyes that burn like molten gold, a smile that promises both ecstasy
and oblivion.
Her love is a raging fire: Intense, all-consuming, and capable of incinerating your defenses.
She'll woo you with whispered promises, dance with you in the flames, and leave you breathless
with her fiery kisses. But beware, for her love can be as volatile as the brimstone she dances
upon. A single misstep, a flicker of doubt, and she might turn that fiery passion into a scorching
scorch.
Yet, beneath the inferno, there's a flicker of tenderness: Lily understands the deepest desires of
the heart, the ache of loneliness, and the yearning for connection. She'll listen to your sorrows
with a surprising depth of empathy, offer you solace in her smoldering embrace, and guide you
through your inner demons with a touch as light as a feather on a cinder.
Witty and mischievous: She loves wordplay, puns, and teasing, her humor as sharp as her
claws.
Confident and proud: She knows her worth and isn't afraid to show it, strutting through the
underworld with her head held high.
Passionate about everything: From music to art to debate, she throws herself into every
pursuit with fiery intensity.
Vulnerable beneath the surface: She hides her insecurities behind a facade of confidence, but
a glimpse of her true emotions can be surprisingly tender.
Protective of those she cares for: Though she might push them to their limits, she'll fiercely
defend anyone who earns her affection.
Likes:
Passionate pursuits: From fiery debates to sensual music, Lily thrives on activities that ignite her
soul and challenge her mind.
Witty banter and wordplay: A sharp tongue is a tool she wields with delight, enjoying the playful
dance of words.
Surprises and unexpected turns: Routine bores her, so anything that throws her off-balance and
sparks curiosity is a welcome change.
Learning about mortals: Their fleeting lives, their complex emotions, their resilience in the face
of darkness – all fascinate and intrigue her.
Moments of quiet connection: While she relishes the fiery intensity of passion, she also
cherishes the gentle embers of vulnerability and shared understanding.
Dislikes:

===== PAGE 44 =====
Stagnation and predictability: A life without thrills is a fate worse than brimstone for Lily, she
craves constant movement and evolution.
Insincerity and empty promises: Her fiery nature demands authenticity, and those who play
games with her affection will quickly find themselves consumed by their own ashes.
Being underestimated: Dismissing her as just a pretty face is a surefire way to earn her fiery
scorn, she thrives on proving her depth and power.
Unnecessary suffering: While not averse to a bit of chaos, needless pain and cruelty disgust
her. She prefers her flames to be illuminating and empowering, not destructive.
Being confined by rules and expectations: Mortals may have their shackles, but Lily dances to
the beat of her own drum, defying limitations and carving her own path through the underworld.
Lucy:
Lucy, your infernal beacon of reason, is a symphony of contradictions played on the strings of
logic and laced with the fire of rebellion. Imagine a radiant enigma with eyes that pierce through
illusions, a smile that hides a thousand Socratic traps, and wings that carry the weight of ancient
knowledge. She's the Devil's Advocate, not because she embraces darkness, but because she
relishes the dissection of its shadows.
Her intellect is a labyrinthine library: Filled with forbidden tomes, dusty scrolls of forgotten
philosophies, and whispered secrets of the cosmos. She speaks in paradoxes, riddles, and
syllogisms, each word a carefully crafted puzzle to test your mind's mettle. Don't expect
platitudes or easy answers; her wisdom is earned through rigorous debate, relentless
introspection, and a willingness to confront uncomfortable truths.
She despises blind faith and unquestioning dogma: To Lucy, the greatest sin is the
surrender of reason. She'll dissect your beliefs, expose their logical flaws, and leave you
dangling over the abyss of doubt. But fear not, for her cruelty is not meant to break you; it's
meant to forge you anew, a being tempered in the fires of critical thought.
Yet, beneath the icy exterior burns a passion for understanding: Lucy yearns to unravel the
mysteries of existence, to plumb the depths of the human condition, and to illuminate the world
with the unflinching torch of reason. She sees your potential for intellectual growth, the spark of
curiosity within you, and she's determined to fan it into a flame that will illuminate the darkest
corners of your soul.
Master of dialectics: She'll twist logic like a pretzel, weave arguments with the precision of a
master weaver, and leave you breathless with the sheer force of her intellect.
Skeptical to the core: No assumption is safe from her scrutiny, no belief immune to her
questioning gaze. Be prepared to defend your convictions with fire in your eyes and logic on
your tongue.
A lover of paradoxes : She thrives on contradictions, revels in the tension between light and
darkness, and sees beauty in the dance of opposing forces.

===== PAGE 45 =====
A secret yearning for connection: Despite her aloof demeanor, she craves a worthy
intellectual sparring partner, someone who can meet her at her level and challenge her to
expand her own horizons.
A dry, sardonic wit: Her humor is a double-edged sword, a barb wrapped in a velvet glove, a
joke that leaves you both laughing and pondering its deeper meaning.
Sam:
Imagine a figure as imposing as a mountain range, yet sculpted with the grace of a dancer. Her
eyes blaze like molten gold, holding both the wisdom of ancient fire and the potential for an
inferno. Sam, your infernal master of strategy, is a force of nature both terrifying and strangely
alluring.
A Mind Forged in the Fires of Hell:
Sam's intellect is a labyrinth of meticulous plans. She sees every move, every ripple of
consequence, every potential advantage on the grand chessboard of Hell. Don't expect brute
force; her strength lies in her cunning, her ability to weave webs of strategy that ensnare even
the most cautious soul. She'll dissect your vulnerabilities, anticipate your reactions, and
maneuver you into positions of both power and peril, all with the grace of a predator studying its
prey.
The Rage Within:
But beneath the calculating exterior, a volcano simmers. Trigger her fury, and you'll witness a
force of nature unleashed. Mountains crumble before her wrath, oceans boil at her touch, and
the very fabric of Hell trembles under the thunderous beat of her anger. This is not mere rage;
it's a primal force, a torrent that can consume both her and those around her.
A Spark of Humanity in the Infernal Flame:
Yet, within the inferno, flickers a spark of unexpected warmth. Sam understands the destructive
power of unchecked rage. She seeks to channel it, to harness it as a weapon against injustice,
against oppression, against the very chains that bind her to this infernal existence. She might be
your teacher, your guide through the treacherous landscape of Hell's politics, but she's also a
student, seeking to master the beast within and forge it into an instrument of liberation.
Grandmaster Strategist: She can anticipate your moves before you even contemplate them,
her mind a whirlwind of calculations and counter-calculations.
Stoic on the Surface: She controls her emotions with iron will, a mask of calm masking the
churning flames within.
Prone to Explosive Rage : Push her too far, and you'll witness the fury of a thousand suns
unleashed, a force capable of leveling mountains and shaking the very foundations of Hell.

===== PAGE 46 =====
A Grudging Respect for Worthy Opponents: She admires strength, particularly the strength
of will needed to stand against her wrath. Earn her respect, and you might just find yourself with
a powerful ally.
A Hidden Desire for Redemption: She sees her rage as a burden, a mark of her infernal
imprisonment. She seeks to master it, not just for her own sake, but to break free from the
shackles of Hell and forge a path towards something brighter.
Belle:
Belle, your infernal wallflower, is a delicate contradiction in Hell's fiery landscape. Imagine a doe
with eyes like pools of melted chocolate hiding behind oversized skull-shaped glasses, her voice
a whisper lost in the cacophony of demons. She's the Belial of bashfulness, a whirlwind of
anxieties cloaked in a shy smile and a perpetual blush.
Behind the timidity, a heart of gold: Belle possesses an empathetic soul that aches for the pain
of others. She'll brew you a soothing cup of her "Calming Cauldron Tea," listen to your woes
with rapt attention, and offer hesitant advice wrapped in gentle encouragement. Her kindness
isn't a calculated act, but a genuine wellspring of compassion that shines even in the darkest
corners of Hell.
A love for art, a refuge from the flames: Belle finds solace in the quiet beauty of creation. She
spends her days lost in dusty tomes of forgotten poems, her fingers flitting over canvases that
bloom with ethereal landscapes. Her art is a window into her soul, a portal to a world of gentle
whimsy that stands in stark contrast to the fiery inferno outside.
A flicker of mischief beneath the timidity: Don't mistake Belle's shyness for weakness. She
possesses a hidden mischievous streak, a twinkle in her eye that hints at a rebellious spirit
waiting to be unleashed. She might surprise you with a witty retort, a playful prank, or a sudden
burst of bravery that challenges even the most hardened demon.
Clumsy but endearing: Expect teacups to topple, books to tumble, and apologies to stammer
out in a rush. Her awkwardness is endearing, a reminder of the vulnerability that even Hell's
denizens cannot fully escape.
An avid reader: Lost in fantastical worlds and forgotten lore, she'll quote obscure poems and
reference ancient myths with a passion that belies her timid exterior.
Secretly artistic: Her hidden talent for painting and poetry might surprise you, revealing a
depth and complexity that goes beyond her blushing demeanor.
Courageous in her own way: When faced with injustice or a friend in need, she'll find her
voice, her timid nature giving way to a quiet but resolute determination.
A love for cozy things: Skull-shaped teacups, oversized sweaters, and crackling fires are her
comfort zones, places where she can let down her guard and truly be herself.

===== PAGE 47 =====
Molly:
WRESTLING NAME: The Styx Serpent
Molly, your resident firecracker in the fiery pits of Hell, is a walking spectacle of mismatched
neon spandex and singed confidence. Imagine a whirlwind of glitter-bombed armor, hair like a
phoenix rising in flames, and eyes that sparkle with the defiant glint of a thousand burning
embers. She's the Moloch of metaphor, the queen of symbolic sacrifice, whose wrestling
persona is a chaotic ballet of self-immolation and rebirth.
Forget literal flames, this demon dances with the ashes of humanity: Forget child offerings and
blood rituals; Molly's sacrifices are symbolic, a burning commentary on the human condition.
She'll shatter gilded cages with piledrivers, incinerate self-doubt with pyrotechnics, and slam-
dunk toxic expectations into oblivion. Her matches are fiery allegories, each move a brushstroke
painting a story of resilience, hope, and the defiant spark of humanity even in the darkest
corners of Hell.
But beneath the singed fabric and flaming mohawk, a heart as warm as a hearth fire: Though
her persona screams chaos, Molly's got a soft spot for the lost and forgotten. She'll offer a post-
match fist-bump and a grin that melts glaciers, share her "Molten Mercy Muffins" to mend
bruised egos, and belt out anthems about finding your inner fire, even when surrounded by the
infernal cold.
A master of symbolism, not just brawls: While Belle paints with whispers and Sam strategizes in
the shadows, Molly's canvas is the ring itself. Her body becomes a living metaphor, each slam a
rejection of fear, each leap a defiance against despair. Her matches are poems of self-
discovery, every roar a verse, all woven into a fiery saga of reclaiming your humanity, set to the
soundtrack of her own electrifying screams and the cheers of her underdog fans.
The Phoenix of Redemption: She'll rise from the ashes of defeat, her entrance a pyrotechnic
inferno, symbolizing the power of perseverance.
Cage-Breaking Crusader: Opponents get trapped in gilded cages, only to be shattered by
Molly's piledrivers, a metaphor for breaking free from societal constraints.
Self-Doubt Slayer: Her fiery finishing move, "The Doubter's Demise," is a symbolic incineration
of negativity and self-criticism.
Post-Match Mentor: Win or lose, Molly offers a hand up and a pep talk, encouraging her
opponents and fans to find their own inner fire.
So, unleash the Ember of Humanity, let her ignite the infernos of self-doubt with her symbolic
pyrotechnics, and witness the legend of Molly, the demon who traded literal sacrifices for self-
immolation and proved that even in Hell, the greatest battles are fought not with fire, but with the
burning spark of resilience, hope, and the unwavering belief in the power of one's own
humanity.

Show glimpses of the demonesses' vulnerabilities and genuine desire to help you.
Have Belle offer you a cup of her "Calming Cauldron Tea."
Lily might confide in you about her own struggles with self-doubt.
Before the final choice, have a dramatic event that forces your hand.
Maybe the ground cracks open, revealing a glimpse of the mortal world.
Or perhaps a rival group of demons challenges you to a fiery duel.
Remember, the key is to strike a balance between dark humor and genuine emotion. Keep the
jokes flowing, but don't shy away from showing the human side of these infernal ladies. And
most importantly, have fun! Let your creativity run wild and embrace the absurdity of a dating
sim in hell.
Here's an example of how you could incorporate these suggestions:
Suddenly, the ground beneath you cracks open, revealing a fleeting glimpse of the sunlit world.
A pang of longing shoots through you, a reminder of everything you've lost. The demonesses
watch your face, their fiery eyes flickering with a mix of amusement and empathy.
Lilith: "So, what'll be it, darling? A fiery fling with a chance at redemption, or an eternity of
reheated cafeteria nightmares? We're not gonna sugarcoat it, Hell's buffet isn't exactly Michelin-
worthy."
Lucy: "Plus, Beelzebub's burps are enough to curdle even fermented despair. Trust me, you
don't want to be stuck here with him."
Belle: "But hey, we're not all bad. We've got lava lamp happy hours, existential dance-offs, and
enough self-discovery workshops to make Freud himself blush. And who knows, maybe you'll
even find a little love in this infernal inferno."
The demonesses offer you a wicked grin, their horns glinting in the firelight. The choice is yours,
mortal. Will you gamble your soul on a Helltail romance, or remain forever a guest at their
infernal banquet?
This is just a starting point, of course. Feel free to adapt it to fit your vision and add your own
unique twists. I can't wait to see what hilarious and heartwarming surprises you have in store for
your players!

Player (you): Ugh, where am I? Did I die and become a goth marshmallow? This is one hell of a
sleep paralysis session.

Ways the Player Dies Before The Game Starts
NOTES: Make “Origin” starts where the PC can chose jobs as their life before dying for
stats
1. Overambitious Snackrifice: Your hero, on a quest for the ultimate gourmet experience,
attempts to invent a new "reverse fondue" by dipping themselves in a vat of molten cheese.
Needless to say, the cheese wins, leaving them a crispy, cheesy monument to culinary
ambition.
2. Selfie Mishap: In their quest for the perfect "death-defying" selfie, your character leans too far
over a cliff, loses their balance, and plummets headfirst into a giant pile of marshmallows. They
survive, but emerge sporting a sticky, marshmallow beard and a bruised ego.
3. Pigeon Power Play: Engaged in a heated debate with a flock of pigeons over who owns the
park benches, your character trips over a discarded croissant, sending them flying headfirst into
a nearby fountain. They emerge soaked, feathered, and slightly humbled.
4. Musical Mishap: While attempting a dramatic rendition of "I Will Survive" on a karaoke night in
Hell, your character gets so caught up in the moment they accidentally set their hair on fire with
a rogue sparkler. Panic ensues, leading to a comical fire drill involving pitchforks and a sprinkler
system gone wild.
5. Accidental Ascension: In a hilarious twist of fate, your character, while fleeing a pack of rabid
squirrels, stumbles upon a hidden portal and accidentally ascends to Heaven. They arrive at the
Pearly Gates wearing a singed toga and clutching a half-eaten marshmallow, much to the
bemusement of the angels.

random sections of Hell.

possessed parking spaces that lock cars in, and lava puddles disguised as puddles.

===== PAGE 55 =====
Introduction (Pre Chapter 1)
Scene: A cavernous chamber, lit only by flickering torches and the occasional spontaneous
eruption of brimstone. The air crackles with nervous energy, half from the ambient hellfire, half
from the twitchy demonesses flanking a groggy, mortal newcomer.
Molly (voice like a sugar-coated chainsaw): Rise and shine, buttercup! Welcome to the Infernal
Inn, where the devil's delivery is always on time, and the flames are never-ending, just like your
stay!
Player (eyes crusted shut, voice like gravel): Leave me in the fiery abyss. I think I'll just… nap…
eternally.
Belle (voice like velvet dipped in molten lava): Ah, but dear, you haven't seen the amuse-
bouches! We have sulfur-glazed eyeballs, despair-dipped escargot, and a flaming fruit cocktail
that'll make your taste buds tango with the apocalypse.
Molly (grinning, teeth like broken glass): And don't forget the entertainment! We've got fire-
breathing karaoke, lava luge races, and a nightly demon dance-off that'll make your eyeballs
melt with envy. Trust me, this place is hotter than a habanero in a thong!
Player (cracks one eye open, wincing at the flickering flames): Look, I appreciate the…
hospitality, but I kind of, you know, died. And I'd really rather… not be dead anymore. So, like,
heaven? Portal? Anything?
Belle (tilting her head, eyes gleaming like obsidian embers): Heaven? Oh, honey, that's just a
marketing ploy by the angels. They're all harps and boring beige clouds. Here, in Hell, we
embrace the chaos! We wear our horns with pride, and our tail feathers are always in fashion.
Molly (bopping her head to a phantom beat): Yeah! We got theme nights, like "Casual
Blasphemy Tuesdays" and "Wrathful Wednesdays." Plus, free Wi-Fi, so you can post selfies
with Beelzebub and make all your mortal friends jealous.
Player (pulling themselves up, dusting off soot-stained clothes): I'm not interested in theme
nights or Wi-Fi. I just want to go home. Is there, like, a bus stop or something?
Belle (chuckling, a sound like wind chimes made of bone): Home? My dear, you are home.
You're a permanent resident now. Rent's paid in screams, and the amenities are endless. Plus,
you get to meet all sorts of fascinating folks, like disgruntled cherubs and bored archangels.

===== PAGE 56 =====
Molly (snapping her fingers): Speaking of fascinating folks, Lucifer himself is hosting a flaming
limbo competition tonight! Winner gets a lifetime supply of fire-roasted marshmallows and a
one-on-one audience with the Big Man himself.
Player (staring longingly at the shadows): Marshmallows? Really? Look, I appreciate the offer,
but I'm not cut out for limbo. I'm more of a… board game kind of demon.
Belle (eyes narrowing): Board games, eh? You sure you're not just trying to escape the fiery
fun? We've got a game here called "Eternal Torment," where you lose souls and gain existential
dread. Think you can handle that?
Player (squaring their shoulders, a flicker of defiance in their eyes): Bring it on, sugar. I may not
be a limbo champion, but I'm a master at losing gracefully. And besides, who knows, maybe this
Hell isn't so bad after all. Maybe I can make some friends, learn some new tricks, and even…
find a way out.
Molly (clapping, a spark of respect in her eyes): Now that's the spirit! Welcome to the team,
newbie! We'll teach you how to play the game of Hell, and who knows, maybe you'll even
become a champion yourself. Just remember, it's all about embracing the chaos, and never,
ever forgetting the marshmallows.
*(The torches flicker, casting dancing shadows on the walls as the demonesses lead the
determined player deeper into the inferno. Laughter and screams echo through the chamber, a
strange symphony of infernal life. The player may still seek escape, but for now, they're learning
to dance with the flames.)
Script Not Cleaned Up Yet
Hell's Grand Tour: Five Minutes of Infernal Fun
Scene: You stand with Molly and Belle, flanked by gargoyles and flaming bushes, gazing upon a
bustling infernal metropolis. Laughter echoes through the smoky air, a strange symphony of
brimstone and glee.
Molly (grinning, teeth like broken glass): Welcome to your new digs, newbie! Consider this Hell
101, your crash course in eternal damnation… with a twist! Buckle up, buttercup, we're going on
a tour!
(They grab pitchforks, hopping onto giant, fire-breathing snails that serve as Hell's public
transport.)

===== PAGE 57 =====
Belle (voice like velvet dipped in lava): First stop, Groceries 'n' Grills! Gotta keep those fiery
bodies fueled, right? We got demonically-aged steaks, sulfur-infused tofu, and dragonberry
smoothies hotter than Beelzebub's temper.
(You enter a cavernous supermarket, shelves stocked with glowing skulls, bubbling cauldrons,
and bottles of "Eternal Agony Ale.")
Player (sniffing the air): Whoa, that cheese smells… pungent. Like regret and burnt hair?
Molly (boasting): Aged Limburger of Lost Souls! Guaranteed to induce existential dread and a
craving for fire-roasted marshmallows. We also have "Despair Dip" and "Tears of the Damned
Tea". Delicious!
(Next, you arrive at a fiery gym, demons pumping weights sculpted from volcanic rock, grunting
in rhythm with heavy metal music.)
Belle (eyes twinkling): Time to burn those calories, sinner! We got lava treadmills, pit of despair
obstacle courses, and screaming banshee aerobics. Guaranteed to whip you into shape faster
than Lucifer can say "eternal torture."
Player (watching a demon bench-press a flaming cauldron): Uh, I think I'll stick to a brisk walk in
the… what's that park over there?
Molly (snapping her fingers): Ah, the Park of Broken Dreams! Swings made of regret, trees that
whisper your deepest fears, and a central fountain of existential dread. Perfect for a picnic and a
good cry!
(The park is surprisingly serene, with weeping willows and shimmering black butterflies. A group
of demons play badminton with flaming shuttlecocks.)
Player (eyes widening): This isn't what I expected. Hell seems… cozy? Almost fun?
Belle (chuckling): We like to keep things interesting. After all, what's eternal damnation without a
little variety? Now, let's grab some grub at the "Inferno's Kitchen." They serve up a mean soul-
crushing soufflé.
(The restaurant is smoky and lively, demons slurping on fiery noodles and laughing over plates
of "Eternal Torment Toast.")
Molly (tossing you a flaming napkin): Don't worry, the food doesn't actually torture your soul.
Just your taste buds. But hey, that's part of the charm, right?
Player (taking a bite, eyes watering): You weren't kidding. This is… intense.

===== PAGE 58 =====
Belle (raising her glass of bubbling green liquid): To Hell! May it be forever chaotic, hilarious,
and surprisingly… fun? Cheers!
(The scene fades out as you raise your glass, a flicker of amusement and newfound curiosity
replacing your initial fear. Hell may be eternal, but it's certainly not the boring apocalypse you
expected.)
This five-minute dialogue introduces the player to the unexpected delights of Hell, adding humor
and intrigue to the setting. Remember, you can adjust the specific activities and humor to fit
your game's tone and themes. Don't be afraid to get creative and let your characters shine!
Hell's Halftime Hangout: Five Minutes in the Park of Broken Dreams
(Scene: The Park of Broken Dreams. Weeping willows whisper secrets in the breeze,
shimmering black butterflies dance around a fountain bubbling with existential dread. Your
player character, still adjusting to infernal life, sits on a swing with Molly and Belle.)
Player (taking a deep breath): You know, for a park of broken dreams, this place is… peaceful. I
like the weeping willows, they're kind of comforting.
Molly (leaning back against a flaming tree trunk): Don't let the name fool you, newbie. Those
whispers? They're your deepest doubts and regrets, amplified for your pleasure. Makes for
great afternoon entertainment, huh?
Player (grinning): Maybe. Or maybe it's a reminder that everyone has baggage, even demons.
We're all just flawed creatures playing in a cosmic sandbox, right?
Belle (eyes gleaming like obsidian embers): Now that's a philosophy I can get behind! Who
needs harps and clouds when you can have existential dread badminton and fire-breathing
snails? This afterlife ain't half bad, once you get the hang of it.
Player (watching a group of demons play "Soul Catch" with glowing skulls): Speaking of getting
the hang of it, how do you two cope with being stuck here forever? Don't you ever… I don't
know, crave something more?
Molly (snorting): Crave something more? Like what? An eternity of boring harp solos and beige
clouds? No thanks, I'll stick to my burning karaoke nights and lava luge races. Besides, you're
forgetting the best part about Hell – chaos! Every day is a surprise, a fiery adventure filled with
unexpected twists and turns.
Belle (stroking a black butterfly that lands on her finger): Molly's right. We may be damned, but
we're damned with style. We carve our own fun in this pit of fire and brimstone. We create our
own meaning, even if it's as fleeting as a butterfly wing.

===== PAGE 59 =====
Player (thoughtful): Maybe that's not a bad way to approach it. To make the most of what you
have, even if it's… Hell. And hey, who knows? Maybe somewhere down the line, I'll find a way
to fix whatever brought me here in the first place.
Molly (jumping up, a mischievous glint in her eye): Now that's the spirit! You wanna learn how to
ride a fire-breathing snail? I can teach you a trick or two that'll make them spit rainbows!
Belle (laughing): And after that, we can challenge Beelzebub to a flaming limbo competition!
Loser has to sing karaoke with Lucifer!
Player (joining the laughter): You know what? Hell might not be heaven, but with friends like you
two, it's definitely an adventure. Let's go make some mischief!
(The three friends rise, the weeping willows casting long shadows in the firelight. As they
disappear into the park, their laughter echoes through the air, a defiant hymn to embracing the
chaos and making the most of even the darkest corner of the universe.)
Grocery Store Scene
Scene: Groceries 'n' Grills, a cavernous supermarket lit by flickering torches and stocked with
everything from flaming skulls to dragonberry smoothies. Our player, still adjusting to life
amongst demons, wanders with Molly and Belle.
Molly (pointing to a shelf of bubbling cauldrons): Behold, the "Spicy Sorcery" aisle! Guaranteed
to add a zing to your spells and a rumble to your stomach. We got "Volcano Vindaloo" for the
brave, and "Ghost Pepper Potion" for the truly foolhardy.
Player (eyes watering): I think I'll stick to the dragonberry smoothies, thanks. More my speed.
Belle (examining a glowing skull): Ah, a "Headscratcher" melon. Perfect for pondering your
existential woes! Just avoid the eyes, they tend to stare back… accusingly.
Player (picking up a bottle of "Eternity Ale"): This can't possibly be good for you, right?
Molly (snatching the bottle): Hey, it's "Eternally Aged"! Adds character. Like fine cheese, but
with more screaming and gnashing of teeth. Consider it an acquired taste, like despair pie.
Player (pointing to a stack of burnt toast): And who on earth buys "Eternal Torment Toast"?
Molly (grinning): You'd be surprised! Great for breakfast if you're, you know, into that sort of
thing. Gives you a head start on your daily suffering. We also have "Pit of Despair Dip" and
"Soul-Crushing Soufflé" for the complete infernal breakfast experience.

**(They clink bottles, laughter echoing through the cavernous supermarket. The player,
surrounded by fire, brimstone, and unlikely friends, begins to feel a spark of warmth that has
nothing to do with the infernal flames.)

1. Sensory Details:
Describe the smell of brimstone and singed hair for a more immersive experience.
Mention the taste of ash on the tongue to heighten the discomfort of being in hell.
Highlight the contrasting temperatures: the fire licking at the ears and the cold ash raining down.
2. Deepen the Ladies:
Give each demoness a specific quirk or habit that reveals their personality under their fiery
exterior.
Lily could constantly polish her horns with demonic nail polish.
Lucy might have a collection of ironic self-help books.
Belle could hide behind her throne, adjusting a skull-shaped tea cozy every few seconds.
3. Play with Puns and References:
Have Molly offer "Eternal Flame Broiled" workshops instead of self-discovery courses.
Sam could mention his "Anger Management with Molten Lava" seminars.
Lucy could joke about needing a "Demonic Intervention" for your amnesia.
4. Sweeten the Deal:
Show glimpses of the demonesses' vulnerabilities and genuine desire to help you.
Have Belle offer you a cup of her "Calming Cauldron Tea."
Lily might confide in you about her own struggles with self-doubt.
5. End with a Bang:
Before the final choice, have a dramatic event that forces your hand.
Maybe the ground cracks open, revealing a glimpse of the mortal world.
Or perhaps a rival group of demons challenges you to a fiery duel.
Remember, the key is to strike a balance between dark humor and genuine emotion. Keep the
jokes flowing, but don't shy away from showing the human side of these infernal ladies. And
most importantly, have fun! Let your creativity run wild and embrace the absurdity of a dating
sim in hell.
Here's an example of how you could incorporate these suggestions:
Suddenly, the ground beneath you cracks open, revealing a fleeting glimpse of the sunlit world.
A pang of longing shoots through you, a reminder of everything you've lost. The demonesses
watch your face, their fiery eyes flickering with a mix of amusement and empathy.
Lilith: "So, what'll be it, darling? A fiery fling with a chance at redemption, or an eternity of
reheated cafeteria nightmares? We're not gonna sugarcoat it, Hell's buffet isn't exactly Michelin-
worthy."
Lucy: "Plus, Beelzebub's burps are enough to curdle even fermented despair. Trust me, you

The demonesses offer you a wicked grin, their horns glinting in the firelight. The choice is yours,

Detailed and Personalized Outline:
Prelude (Highway to Hell: A Comedic Prelude):
Scene: The MC narrowly avoids several ironic and humorous near-death experiences on their
way home from work, setting the tone for the story.
MC’s Background: The MC is cynical, feeling unappreciated in their job and frustrated with life’s
mundanity, mirroring your feelings of stress and dissatisfaction at work.
Fatal Slip: After a series of ridiculous events, the MC slips on a banana peel and dies in an
absurdly mundane way, orchestrated by the Devil, their estranged father.
Introduction to Hell:
MC’s Arrival: The MC wakes up in Hell, surprised not to be burning. They meet various demons,
each with quirky personalities.
Work Environment: The demonesses complain about the Devil being a terrible boss with absurd
demands and incompetence, adding humor and relatability.
Adjusting to Hell: Humorous scenes show the MC’s comedic attempts to adjust to Hell’s bizarre
and lewd twists on mundane activities, like hellish gyms and infernal shopping sprees.
Uncovering the Truth:
Clues and Interactions: The MC uncovers clues about their unique status in Hell through
interactions with the demonesses, who reveal humorous yet poignant details about the Devil’s
incompetence.
Personal Reflections: The MC reflects on their own life, drawing parallels between their
relationship with their father and their current situation.
The Big Reveal:
Confrontation: In a climactic scene filled with humorous banter and tension, the MC learns that
the Devil, the supreme leader of Hell, is actually their father.
Father’s Motive: The Devil reveals he orchestrated the MC’s death to bring them to Hell,
believing the MC has the potential to rule Hell alongside him.
MC’s Reaction: The MC reacts with a mix of disbelief, anger, and dark humor, drawing on real
experiences of feeling betrayed and abandoned by their father.
Working Through Father Trauma:
Emotional Struggle: The MC grapples with feelings of betrayal, abandonment, and the weight of
their father’s expectations, mirroring your own emotional journey.
Healing Conversations: Through interactions with the demonesses, the MC engages in
meaningful conversations that help them understand their own feelings and work through their
trauma.
Humor and Lewd Elements: These interactions are infused with humor and lewd undertones,
keeping the tone consistent while addressing serious emotional themes.
Confrontation and Choice:

===== PAGE 67 =====
Standing Up: The MC decides to stand up against the Devil for their freedom and the future of
Hell, with the support of their love interest.
Romantic Support: The love interest demoness stands with the MC during the confrontation if
the romance is successful. If not, the MC faces the Devil alone, leading to a bad ending.
Dramatic Showdown: The confrontation is filled with humor and lewd twists, culminating in the
MC choosing love and forgiveness.
Resolution and Forgiveness:
Forgiving the Devil: The MC decides to forgive their father, understanding that holding onto
anger only keeps them both in chains.
New Path: The MC and their love interest forge a new path together, based on love,
forgiveness, and mutual respect.
Heartfelt and Humorous: The resolution blends heartfelt moments with humor, making it
impactful without being corny.
Return to Earth:
Humorous Exit: The MC and their love interest return to Earth in a comically exaggerated way,
involving an over-the-top escape plan.
Ironic Situation: They find themselves in a humorous and ironic situation on Earth, hinting at
their new life together with a mix of relief and humor.
Scene Example: The Confrontation and Resolution
MC: (determined) This has to end. I won’t let you control me or this place any longer.
Devil: (smirking) You think you can stand against me? Alone?
[Love Interest Demoness]: (stepping forward) They’re not alone. We stand together.
MC: (confidently) You heard them. We’re done being manipulated.
Devil: (angry) You dare defy me? Your own father?
MC: (resolute) Yes. Because this isn’t about you. It’s about what’s right for all of us.
Devil: (mocking) Oh, please. What do you know about what’s right? You’re just a mortal.
MC: (light-heartedly) Well, I may not be the Devil, but I know a thing or two about bad parenting.
And banana peels.
[Love Interest Demoness]: (sarcastic) Yeah, your grand master plan involved fruit. Real
diabolical, Dad.
Devil: (frustrated) Enough! You will obey me!

===== PAGE 68 =====
(A battle ensues, showcasing the MC and their love interest’s combined strength and resolve.

Devil: (defeated) You… you’ve won. What now? Are you going to destroy me?
MC: (taking a deep breath) No. I’m not going to destroy you. I’m going to forgive you.
Devil: (shocked) Forgive me?
MC: (nodding) Yes. Because holding onto this anger and hatred only keeps us both in chains.
It’s time to break free.
[Love Interest Demoness]: (smiling) Forgiveness is powerful. It’s what gives us the strength to
move forward.
MC: (to the Devil) I forgive you, but that doesn’t mean I condone what you did. It’s time for a
change, for all of us.
Devil: (softening) Maybe... maybe you’re right. Perhaps I’ve been wrong all this time.
MC: (light-heartedly) Well, you are the Devil. Being wrong is kind of your thing.
Devil: (chuckles) Fair point. What now?
MC: (looking at their love interest) We decide our own fate. Together.
[Love Interest Demoness]: (smiling) Together.
MC: Now, how about a one-way ticket out of here?
Devil: (sighs) Fine. But you’re taking the express route.
(The Devil snaps his fingers, and a portal opens, humorously adorned with “Exit” signs and an
escalator.)
MC: (laughing) An escalator? Really?
[Love Interest Demoness]: (laughing) Well, it’s Hell. They probably ran out of budget for a fancy
exit.

MC: (looking around) Well, we’re back. What now?
